Proposition 4.11
.

Let
k
be suitably large.
Then for any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
and
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.63)

and for any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
,
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
and
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{j=0}^{i}\lVert\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(j)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}\lesssim_{k,p,i}F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.64)

Proof.

The
\mathfrak{s}=0
,
\mathfrak{s}=1
and
\mathfrak{s}=2
cases have been addressed in
[
30
,
67
,
3
]
respectively. We outline the basic idea here.

Consider the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component. We apply the
r^{p}
estimate (
1
) with
\varphi=\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}
and
\vartheta=0
to equation (
4.10
), and by adding this estimate to the BEAM estimate (
4.16b
) for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component, we prove the global
r^{p}
estimate (
4.63
) for
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
.

We next turn to the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component, and to illustrate the approach in proving the desired estimates, it suffices to consider the most complicated case
\mathfrak{s}=2
and the other cases
\mathfrak{s}=0,1
can be dealt with in a same (but simpler) manner.

First, consider the wave system consisting of the first three subequations of (
3.143
). Each of these subequations can be put into the form of (
1
) with the corresponding inhomogeneous terms

\displaystyle\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(0)})={}

\displaystyle-\frac{4(r^{3}-3Mr^{2}+a^{2}r+a^{2}M)}{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}}\Phi_{-2}^{(1)}=O(r^{-1})\Phi_{-2}^{(1)},

(4.65a)

\displaystyle\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(1)})={}

\displaystyle-\frac{2(r^{3}-3Mr^{2}+a^{2}r+a^{2}M)}{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}}\Phi_{-2}^{(2)}-\frac{6a(r^{2}-a^{2})}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}-\frac{6Mr^{4}-6a^{2}r^{3}-18a^{2}Mr^{2}-6a^{4}r}{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle O(r^{-1})\Phi_{-2}^{(2)}+O(1)\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}+O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)},

(4.65b)

\displaystyle\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(2)})={}

\displaystyle\frac{20a^{2}(r^{3}-3Mr^{2}+a^{2}r+a^{2}M)}{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}}\Phi_{-2}^{(1)}-\frac{8a(r^{2}-a^{2})}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(1)}

\displaystyle-\frac{24a^{3}r}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}-\frac{6a^{2}(r^{4}+10Mr^{3}-6a^{2}Mr-a^{4})}{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(1)}+O(r^{-1})\Phi_{-2}^{(1)}+O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\eta}\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}+O(1)\Phi_{-2}^{(0)}.

(4.65c)

Thus, for each
\varphi=\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}
and
\vartheta=\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(i)})
,
i\in[0,1,2]
, we can apply point (
1
) of Lemma
4.8
to achieve its corresponding
r^{p}
estimate (
1
). It remains to estimate the last term
\lVert\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}
on the RHS of (
1
), which is naturally bounded by
\lVert\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}})}+\sum\limits_{i=0,1,2}\lVert\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{[R_{0}-M,R_{0}]})}
. By adding
A_{i}
multiple of the estimate (
1
) for
\varphi=\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}
and summing over
i=0,1,2
, and by further taking
A_{0}\gg A_{1}\gg A_{2}
, one finds that
\sum_{i=0,1,2}A_{i}\lVert\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(i)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}})}
is absorbed. In the end, adding in the BEAM estimate (
4.16a
) yields the desired estimate (
4.64
) in the case of
i=2
.

For the case
i=3
, we again put the equation (
3.144
) of
\Phi_{-2}^{(3)}
into the form of (
1
) with
\varphi=\Phi_{-2}^{(3)}
and

\displaystyle\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(3)})=\text{the last three lines of equation }\eqref{eq:waveeq:Phiminustwo3}

(4.65d)

and apply the
r^{p}
estimate (
1
). The term
\lVert\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(3)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}
on the RHS of the estimate (
1
) for
\varphi=\Phi_{-2}^{(3)}
is bounded by the spacetime integral on the LHS of the estimate (
4.64
) for
i=2
, hence this proves the estimate (
4.64
) for
i=3
. For
i=4
, equation (
3.144
) of
\Phi_{-2}^{(4)}
is put into the form of (
1
) with
\varphi=\Phi_{-2}^{(4)}
and

\displaystyle\vartheta(\Phi_{-2}^{(4)})=\text{the last four lines of equation }\eqref{eq:waveeq:Phiminustwo4},

(4.65e)

and the remaining steps in the case
i=4
are the same as the ones in the case
i=3
.
∎

Lemma 4.12
.

In the region
r\geq 4M
, we have for a spin
s
scalar
\varphi
that

\displaystyle r^{2}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}V\varphi={}

\displaystyle O(1)\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{s,G}\varphi+\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq 2}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}\varphi.

Proof.

In the expression (
3.3
) of the wave operator
\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{s}
, we use
Y=\frac{r^{2}+a^{2}}{\Delta}\big(2\mathcal{L}_{\xi}+\frac{2a}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}-V\big)
away from horizon to rewrite
r^{2}V\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\varphi
as the desired form.
∎

Proposition 4.13
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
,
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
, and let
k
be suitably large. There are constants
k^{\prime}(j)
and
C_{j}
such that for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
and any
\tau\geq\tau_{0}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p,j,k}{}

\displaystyle\ \langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2-2j+C_{j}\delta+p}F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.74a)

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p,j,k}{}

\displaystyle\ \langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2-2(j+2\mathfrak{s}-i)+C_{j}\delta+p}F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.74b)

Proof.

Note that for any
k
suitably large, we have for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
that
\lVert\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-2}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}\gtrsim_{k,p}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},p-1,\tau,\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\mathrm{d}\tau
for a finite
k^{\prime}
by a simple application of Hardy’s inequality, thus the estimate (
4.63
) yields

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},p-1,\tau,\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.75)

An application of Lemma
2.18
to this estimate then implies that for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\lesssim{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2+\delta+p}F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.76)

This proves the estimate (
4.74
) for
j=0
.

To show the general
j\in\mathbb{N}
case for the estimate (
4.74
), we prove it by induction. Assume it holds for
j
, and we prove the
j+1
case. Recall equation (
4.10
) satisfied by
\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}
, and in view of the formula (
4.12
), we have in the region
r\geq 4M
that
r^{2}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}V\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}=\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq 2}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}
. Therefore, for any
\tau>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217ptF^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\lVert rV\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{2-\delta-2}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-2}(\Sigma_{\tau})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Sigma_{\tau})}+E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+1})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle\lVert r^{2}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}V(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\rVert^{2}_{W_{-\delta-2}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-2}(\Sigma_{\tau})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Sigma_{\tau})}+E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+2,\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2-2j+C_{j}\delta}F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

(4.77)

where in the last step we have used the base assumption.
Further, since
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}
commutes with the TME, the estimates (
4.75
) and (
4.76
) are valid if replacing
\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
by
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
. This together with the above estimate yields

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2+\delta+p}F(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}+\frac{\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}}{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2-2(j+1)+p+C_{j}\delta}F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.78)

which thus completes the induction and proves (
4.74
).

We proceed by proving the estimate (
4.74
) for the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component. By definition, one has
\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{i}\lVert\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(j^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}\gtrsim_{k,p}\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}F^{(i)}(k-1,p-1,\tau,\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})
, hence we have from (
4.64
) that

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}F^{(i)}(k-1,p-1,\tau,\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim_{k,p}F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.79)

An application of Lemma
2.18
to (
4.79
)
yields that for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
and
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
,

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\lesssim_{k,p}\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-2+\delta+p}F^{(i)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.80)

By definition, we have
\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(j^{\prime}+1)}=(r^{2}+a^{2})V\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(j^{\prime})}
, hence for any
i\in[\mathfrak{s}+1,2\mathfrak{s}]
,

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\gtrsim_{k}F^{(i-1)}(k,\delta,\tau,\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.81)

The above two estimates together then prove (
4.74
) for
j=0
. The general
j\in\mathbb{N}
cases are proven in a same manner as the above one in proving the general
j\in\mathbb{N}
cases for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component together with an application of

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\lesssim{}F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

(4.82)

that is similar to (
4.3
) for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component.
∎

4.4.
Energy decay estimates for the modes of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components

Since the BEAM estimates (
4.17
) for the modes will be frequently used, we shall estimate the last two terms of the RHS in each subequation of (
4.17
) and deduce an alternative form of the BEAM estimates for the modes of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components. This is provided in the following lemma.

Lemma 4.14

(Alternative form of BEAM estimates for the modes)
.

Let
j,k\in\mathbb{N}
. For any
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
, there exists a constant
k^{\prime}>0
such that

\displaystyle E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau_{2}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+M^{k}_{\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau_{1}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.83a)

\displaystyle E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau_{2}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+M^{k}_{\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle E^{k}_{\Sigma_{\tau_{1}}}(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}).

(4.83b)

Proof.

This follows from BEAM estimates (
4.17
) and the estimates in Proposition
4.11
with
p=\delta
.
∎

We then derive the wave equations of the modes of the scalars
\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)}
and put them into the form of (
1
) such that the
r^{p}
estimates in Lemma
4.8
can be applied.

Lemma 4.15
.

For any
\ell\geq\mathfrak{s}
and
\mathfrak{s}-s\leq i\leq\ell-s
,
the scalars
(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\ell}
, the
\ell
mode of
\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)}
defined in (
3.157
), and the scalars
(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell}

satisfy the following spin-weighted wave equations

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{s,G}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\ell})=\sum_{n\leq d(i)}\sum_{\mathfrak{s}-s\leq j\leq i}O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\ell}+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)}]

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{s,G}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell})=\sum_{n\leq d(i)}\sum_{\mathfrak{s}-s\leq j\leq i}O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell}-\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell-1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)}]

with
d(i)
a constant depending only on
i
.

Further, for
0\leq i\leq\mathfrak{s}-1
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{-\mathfrak{s},G}(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\ell})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\ell}(\vartheta(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}))+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{s}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}],

(4.85i)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{-\mathfrak{s},G}(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\geq\ell}(\vartheta(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}))-\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell-1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{s}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}]

(4.85r)

and for
\mathfrak{s}\leq i\leq 2\mathfrak{s}
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{-\mathfrak{s},G}(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\ell})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\ell}(\vartheta(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}))+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{-\mathfrak{s}}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}],

(4.85aa)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{-\mathfrak{s},G}(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell}={}

\displaystyle\vartheta((\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\ell})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\geq\ell}(\vartheta(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})-\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell-1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell^{\prime}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}].

(4.85aj)

Proof.

We put equation (
3.18
) into the form of (
1
) and find the assumptions in point (
1
) of Lemma
4.8
are all satisfied for
\mathfrak{s}-s\leq i\leq\ell-s
in view of (
2.29
); hence, we arrive at

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\displaystyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\textstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}{\vtop{\halign{\hfil$\scriptscriptstyle#$\hfil\cr\square\cr{\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{S}}\crcr}}}}_{s,G}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle(\hat{H}_{s,i})_{\ell}+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(i)}]

with
(\hat{H}_{s,i})_{\ell}
being the
\ell
mode of
\hat{H}_{s,i}
defined in (
3.166
). By the definition of
\hat{H}_{s,i}
in equation (
3.166
) and using also the expression (
3.157
), one has

\displaystyle(\hat{H}_{s,i})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle\sum_{n\leq d(i)}\sum_{\mathfrak{s}-s\leq j\leq i}O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(j)}.

(4.94)

This together with (
4.4
) proves equation (
4.84
). Equation (
4.84
) follows easily from (
4.84
) and (
3.16
) and using (
3.179
).

The derivation of equations (
4.85
) is direct by applying
\mathbf{P}_{\ell}^{-\mathfrak{s}}
or
\mathbf{P}_{\geq\ell}^{-\mathfrak{s}}
to equations (
4.62
) and making use of the commutator formula (
3.180
).
∎

To apply point (
1
) of the
r^{p}
lemma
4.8
, we have to first estimate the commutator
\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{s}[\varphi_{s}]
for a general spin
s
scalar
\varphi_{s}
. It follows from formula (
3.178
) and Proposition
2.13
that

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{s}[\varphi_{s}]=\sum_{\max\{\mathfrak{s},\ell-2\}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\varphi_{s})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\max\{\mathfrak{s},\ell-1\}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell+1}O(1)(\varphi_{s})_{\ell^{\prime}}.

(4.95)

As a consequence, we obtain

Lemma 4.16
.

Let
k\in\mathbb{N}
. There exists a universal constant
k^{\prime}
such that for any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\tilde{\ell}}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}]\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},\,\,\forall\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\},

(4.96a)

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\tilde{\ell}}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)}]\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},\,\,\forall\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\},

(4.96b)

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{s}[\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+2)}]\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\tilde{\Phi}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}.

(4.96c)

Proof.

Since from Proposition
3.16
, the governing equations of
\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}
and
\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+i)}
are of the same form, it suffices to prove only for the case
s=+\mathfrak{s}
, and a similar argument holds for the
s=-\mathfrak{s}
case.

By (
4.95
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}]={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+1}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell^{\prime}},\quad\forall{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1\},

(4.97a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)}]={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+1}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell^{\prime}},\quad\forall{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1\},

(4.97b)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)}]={}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)}]=\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\mathfrak{s}+3}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\ell^{\prime}}.

(4.97c)

In view of
\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]=-\mathbf{C}_{\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]-\mathbf{C}_{\mathfrak{s}+1}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}]={}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}],

(4.98a)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)}]={}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)}].

(4.98b)

The estimate (
4.96a
) follows from (
4.97a
) and (
4.98a
).

By the formula (
3.157
) of
\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)}
, we have
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\hat{\mathcal{V}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}+\sum_{n\leq c}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}
, and using the formula (
4.12
) together with the wave equation (
4.84
) of
(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}
, we find that there exists a
n>0
such that for any
\ell\geq\mathfrak{s}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell}={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}]

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\max\{\mathfrak{s},\ell-2\}}^{\ell+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\max\{\mathfrak{s},\ell-1\}}^{\ell+1}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}.

(4.99a)

In a similar manner, we conclude that there exists a constant
n>0
such that for any
\ell\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1\}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)}]={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\ell+1}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell^{\prime}}=\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell+2}\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell^{\prime}}.

(4.100)

This together with (
4.98b
) then yields the estimate (
4.96b
).

Finally, by the formula (
3.157
) of
\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)}
, the formula (
4.12
), and the wave equation (
4.84
) of
(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\mathfrak{s}}={}

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\hat{\mathcal{V}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{n\leq n_{1}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}\hat{\mathcal{V}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{i=0,1}\sum_{n\leq n_{2}}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}^{n}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\mathfrak{s}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{1}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\mathcal{V}}\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{2}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}+1}^{\mathfrak{s}+3}\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{3}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}.

(4.101)

This way of arguing can also be employed to eventually achieve

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)}]={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\mathfrak{s}+3}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{2}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\ell^{\prime}}+\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\mathfrak{s}}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}O(1)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\ell^{\prime}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{1}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}}^{\mathfrak{s}+3}\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{2}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)})_{\ell}+\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}+1}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n_{3}}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\ell}.

(4.102)

The estimate (
4.96
) then holds.
∎

In addition, we shall utilize equation (
3.190
) to derive further energy decay for the modes. This is realized by applying the statement in point (
2
) of Lemma
4.8
to equation (
3.190
) for an extended range of
p
. Consequently, we shall estimate the integral term
\lVert\vartheta\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}
or
\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert\vartheta\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau
(by taking
\varepsilon=\delta
) in the estimate (
4.47
) but now with
\vartheta=\tilde{H}_{s,\ell}
that is of the form (
3.19
). The following lemma is to bound these integral terms.

Lemma 4.17
.

For
p\in[\delta,4-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{H}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},

(4.103a)

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{H}_{s,\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}

\displaystyle+\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\tilde{\Phi}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},

(4.103b)

and for
p\in[4+\delta,5-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert\tilde{H}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-6}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau.

(4.104)

Proof.

By the expression of
\tilde{H}_{s,\ell}
in formula (
3.19
), the estimates (
4.103a
) and (
4.104
) follow immediately and we have in addition

\displaystyle\lVert\tilde{H}_{s,\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}

\displaystyle+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert{\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}.

(4.105)

Note that by definition of
\tilde{\Phi}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}
in (
3.189
) and definition of
\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)}
in (
3.157
), one has

\displaystyle(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}}=O(1)\tilde{\Phi}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+2}\sum_{\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert\leq n}O(1)\mathbb{D}^{\mathbf{a}}(\hat{\Phi}_{s}^{(\mathfrak{s}-s)})_{\ell}.

(4.106)

Substituting this back into (
4.4
) then proves the estimate (
4.103
).
∎

Recall from Definition
4.9
the formulas of the
r
-weighted energies

F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\ell})

and
F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau,(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\ell})
for an
\ell
mode and
F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\ell})

and
F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau,(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\ell})
for
\geq\ell
modes of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components, with
p\in[-1,2-\delta]
. For our purpose of deriving extended
r^{p}
hierarchy, we define the following
r
-weighted energies with an enlarged range of the parameter
p
.

Definition 4.18
.

For the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component, define

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})={}

\displaystyle 0,\qquad\text{for }p\in(2-\delta,2+\delta),

(4.107a)

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})={}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}),\,\,\text{for }p\in[2+\delta,5-\delta].

(4.107b)

Define

\displaystyle F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle 0,\qquad\text{for }p\in[-1,\delta)\cup(2-\delta,2+\delta),

(4.108a)

\displaystyle F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle\lVert rV\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-2}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}),\quad\text{for }p\in[\delta,2-\delta],

(4.108b)

\displaystyle F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(1)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}),\,\,\text{for }p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta].

(4.108c)

Define
F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})
for
p\in[-1,2-\delta]
in the same way as in (
4.108
). Further, define

\displaystyle F^{(2)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})={}

\displaystyle 0,\qquad\text{for }p\in[-1,\delta),

(4.109a)

\displaystyle F^{(2)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})={}

\displaystyle\lVert rV\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-2}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle+F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}),\quad\text{for }p\in[\delta,2-\delta].

(4.109b)

For the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component, define

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})={}

\displaystyle 0,\qquad\text{for }p\in(2-\delta,2+\delta),

(4.110a)

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})={}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}),\,\,\text{for }p\in[2+\delta,5-\delta].

(4.110b)

Define

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle 0,\quad\text{for }p\in[-1,\delta)\cup(2-\delta,2+\delta),

(4.111a)

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle\lVert rV\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-2}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=\mathfrak{s}}^{2\mathfrak{s}}F^{(i)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}),\quad\text{for }p\in[\delta,2),

(4.111b)

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})={}

\displaystyle\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}),\,\,\text{for }p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta].

(4.111c)

Define
F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})
for
p\in[-1,2-\delta]
in the same way as in (
4.111
). Further, define

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})={}

\displaystyle 0,\quad\text{for }p\in[-1,\delta),

(4.112a)

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})={}

\displaystyle\lVert rV\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-2}^{k-1}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-2}^{k}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle+F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}),\quad\text{for }p\in[\delta,2).

(4.112b)

Remark 4.19
.

In defining the energies
F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})
and
F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})
for
p\in[2+\delta,5-\delta]
, their expressions are dependent not only on the
\mathfrak{s}
mode of the spin
s
component but also on the other modes in view of the definition (
3.189
) of
\tilde{\Phi}_{s,\mathfrak{s}}
. Similarly for the energies
F^{(1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})
and
F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})
for
p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta]
.

Our first goal is to derive global
r^{p}
estimates for the modes of
\{\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}\}_{i\leq 2\mathfrak{s}}
, which are analogues of the estimates (
4.64
) in Proposition
4.11
but at the mode level.

Corollary 4.20
.

Let
k\in\mathbb{N}
. For any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
,
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
,
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
, and
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-25.83325ptF^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}.

(4.113)

Proof.

The proof is adapted from the one of Proposition
4.11
. The only difference lies in the extra coupling terms with the other modes. It suffices to consider
j=0
case, since
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}
commutes with the TME.
Equations of
(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})})_{\tilde{\ell}}

(i^{\prime}=0,1,\ldots,2\mathfrak{s}
) in system (
4.85
) are the same as the governing equations (
4.62
) of
\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}
except that on the RHS of the wave equations for
(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})})_{\tilde{\ell}}
in system (
4.85
), there is an additional term
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\tilde{\ell}}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}]
. Thus, in applying the
r^{p}
estimate (
1
) to each subequation of
(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})})_{\tilde{\ell}}
, we have one additional integral term
\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\tilde{\ell}}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}[\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}]\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\lesssim_{k,p}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}
. In the end, we combine the obtained
r^{p}
estimates for modes with the BEAM estimates (
4.83
) for modes to conclude the global
r^{p}
estimate (
4.20
).
∎

We then derive the global
r^{p}
estimates for a larger ranger of
p
weight. This is achieved in the following two corollaries.

Corollary 4.21

(Global
r^{p}
estimates for
p\in(0,2)
)
.

There exists a constant
k^{\prime}
such that for any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
, the following global
r^{p}
estimates for
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
hold:

•

for any
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})},

(4.114a)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})};

(4.114b)

•

for any
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(1)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})},

(4.114c)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})};

(4.114d)

•

for
\geq\mathfrak{s}+2
modes,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},

(4.114e)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}.

(4.114f)

Proof.

We take the case
\tilde{\ell}=\mathfrak{s}
of the estimate (
4.114
) as an example to illustrate the general idea. By applying the estimate of point (
1
) in Lemma
4.8
to equation (
4.84
) with
s=+\mathfrak{s}
and
\ell=\mathfrak{s}
and adding in a sufficiently large multiple of the BEAM estimate (
4.83b
) for
\ell=\mathfrak{s}
such that the error terms supported on
[R_{0}-M,R_{0}]
in the
r^{p}
estimate are absorbed, we arrive at

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\mathbf{C}_{{\ell}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}[\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}]\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}.

Note that in the derivation of the above estimate, the error terms arising from the terms with
O(r^{-1})
coefficients on the RHS of (
4.84
) are bounded by
\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}
which has been already controlled in the BEAM estimate. We then make use of the estimate (
4.96a
) to estimate the last term, thus the estimate (
4.114
) with
\tilde{\ell}=\mathfrak{s}
follows. The remaining estimates for the modes of the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component hold by arguing in the same manner by applying the estimate of point (
1
) in Lemma
4.8
to equation (
4.84
), adding in the BEAM estimate (
4.83b
) and making use of the estimate (
4.96
).

As can be seen from Proposition
3.18
, the scalar
(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+i)})_{\tilde{\ell}}
satisfies basically the same wave equation as the one of the scalar
(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\tilde{\ell}}
. Therefore, the above discussions for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component can be applied to prove the desired estimates for the modes of the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component with the only difference that we shall now add in the BEAM estimate (
4.83a
) instead.
∎

Corollary 4.22

(Global
r^{p}
estimates for an extended range of
p
)
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
. There exists a constant
k^{\prime}=k^{\prime}
such that for any
\tau_{2}>\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0}
,

•

for any
p\in[2+\delta,4)
,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})},

(4.115a)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{{\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},

(4.115b)

and for
p\in[4,5-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{{\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}+\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(0)}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-6}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau,

(4.116a)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{{\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}{\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert{\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-6}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau;

(4.116b)

•

for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]\cup[2+\delta,4-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(1)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})},

(4.117a)

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-5-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,p}{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k+k^{\prime},p,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{0\leq i^{\prime}\leq 2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i^{\prime})}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}})}

\displaystyle+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k+k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}.

(4.117b)

Proof.

Note that equation (
3.190
) for
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\ell}
can be put into the form of

\displaystyle\mu Y_{G}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\ell}=\vartheta(\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\ell})=\tilde{H}_{+\mathfrak{s},\ell}.

(4.118)

The proof is based on applying the statement in point (
2
) of Lemma
4.8
to this inhomogeneous transport equation of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\ell}
for an extended range of
p
. Consider first
\ell=\mathfrak{s}
. We apply the
r^{p}
estimate (
4.47
) for
p\in[2+\delta,4)
to equation (
3.190
) of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}
. Note that
\vartheta(\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}})=\tilde{H}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}
and that
\lVert\tilde{H}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}
has been estimated in (
4.103a
), then the estimate (
4.115
) follows by adding in the estimate (
4.114
) with
\tilde{\ell}=\mathfrak{s}
and
p=2-\delta
. This also works for
\mathfrak{s}+1
mode and yields the estimate (
4.117
). To show the estimate (
4.116
) for
p\in[4,5-\delta]
, the only difference from proving (
4.115
) for
p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta]
is that we utilize the
r^{p}
estimate (
4.47
) to equation (
3.190
) of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}
and use the estimate (
4.104
) to bound the error term
\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert\vartheta(\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1})\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau=\int_{\tau_{1}}^{\tau_{2}}\tau^{1+\delta}\lVert\tilde{H}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-4}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}^{\geq R_{0}-M})}\mathrm{d}\tau
.
∎

The above three corollaries on the global
r^{p}
estimates for different modes can be combined together to yield suitable decay for the
r
-weighted energies of the modes.

Definition 4.23
.

For any
k
suitably large and
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
small, define two energies for the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components respectively:

\displaystyle\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]\doteq{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,5-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle+F^{(1)}(k,4-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+F^{(2)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}),

(4.119)

\displaystyle\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}]\doteq{}

\displaystyle F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,5-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle+F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}(k,4-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}).

(4.120)

Similarly define
\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]
and
\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}]
by simply replacing
\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
and
\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}
by
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
and
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}
respectively everywhere. Finally, define

\displaystyle\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau}\doteq\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}]+\textbf{I}^{k,\delta}_{\text{total},\tau}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.121)

Proposition 4.24

(Energy decay for the modes)
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
.
For the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component, we have for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-6-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}],

(4.122a)

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-6-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}],

(4.122b)

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-5-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.122c)

Meanwhile, for any
p\in[2+\delta,5-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-5-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.122d)

For the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component, we have for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})+\sum_{i=0}^{\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\mathfrak{s}+1}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-6-2\mathfrak{s}-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}],

(4.123a)

\displaystyle F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+\sum_{i=0}^{\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-6-2\mathfrak{s}-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}],

(4.123b)

\displaystyle F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i=0}^{\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-5-2\mathfrak{s}-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.123c)

Meanwhile, for any
p\in[2+\delta,5-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-27.98602ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}+\sum_{i=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-5-2j+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.123d)

Proof.

We shall first make use of the global
r^{p}
estimates (
4.20
) for
(\Phi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\tilde{\ell}}

(\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\},i=0,1,\ldots,2\mathfrak{s})
to show some weak decay for the modes of the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component. An application of Lemma
2.18
to the global
r^{p}
estimate (
4.20
) for
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
yields

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\sum_{n=0}^{i}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(n)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}+F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{-2+\delta+p}\Big(F^{(i)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(i)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\Big).

(4.124)

Here, we have made use of the estimate (
4.74
) such that we can add
F^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})
to the LHS.
In addition, we have for any
i\in[\mathfrak{s}+1,2\mathfrak{s}]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-25.83325ptF^{(i-1)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(i-1)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle F^{(i)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(i)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.125)

hence, we utilize these estimates together to obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\sum_{n=0}^{\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(n)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}+F^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(\mathfrak{s}+1)+p-\delta}\big(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(\mathfrak{s}+1)+p-\delta}\big(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\big),

(4.126)

where in the second step we have utilized (
4.82
).
In a similar manner as proving the general
j
case in Proposition
4.13
, it holds

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{1}^{\prime}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)j}(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})),

(4.127)

thus combining the above two estimates with
\tau_{1}^{\prime}=\tau_{1}+\frac{\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}}{2}
then yields for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
and
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
that

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\sum_{n=0}^{\mathfrak{s}}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(n)})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k-\mathfrak{s}-1-l(j,s)}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(\mathfrak{s}+1+j)+p-\delta}(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})).

(4.128)

By the same argument, we have for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
and
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
that

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+p-\delta}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.129)

Next, we consider further decay for the
\mathfrak{s}
mode of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components. Recall the global
r^{p}
estimate (
4.115
) for the
\mathfrak{s}
mode. Consider the case for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component. The estimate (
4.4
) just proven yields that the last three terms on the RHS of (
4.115
) are bounded by
\langle\tau_{1}-\tau_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big)
where
\tau_{1}^{\prime}\in[\tau_{0},\tau_{1}]
being arbitrary, thus an application of Lemma
2.18
to the estimate implies for any
p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(0)}(k,p-2,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-4+\delta+p}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},4-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.130)

Further, because of

\displaystyle\hskip-23.68048ptF^{(0)}(k,4-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j+1}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta}{}

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,2+\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k,\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.131)

by repeating the proof for the general
j
case, we obtain for any
p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k,p-2,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+p-2+C_{j}\delta}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),4-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.132)

By definition (
3.189
) of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}
, there exists a
k^{\prime}>0
such that

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k,2+\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k,\delta,\tau,\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\gtrsim_{\delta,k}F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau,(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},\delta,\tau,\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(4.133)

therefore, the above estimate (
4.4
) together with the previously proven estimate (
4.4
) with
\tilde{\ell}=\mathfrak{s}
implies for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(2+j)+p+C_{j}\delta}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),4-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.134)

Following the same argument, we have for the
\mathfrak{s}
mode of the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component that for any
p\in[2+\delta,4-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{\Phi}_{-\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-5}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k,p-2,\tau_{2},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+p-2+C_{j}\delta}\big(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),4-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\big),

(4.135)

and, together with (
4.4
), we have for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
and
i\in[\mathfrak{s},2\mathfrak{s}]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(i)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i^{\prime}=0}^{i}\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}^{(i)})_{\mathfrak{s}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{-1-\delta}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(2+2\mathfrak{s}-i+j)+p+C_{j}\delta}\big(F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),4-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(2\mathfrak{s})}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.136)

Turn to the
\mathfrak{s}+1
and
\geq\mathfrak{s}+2
modes of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components. Let
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
. In the estimate (
•
‣
4.21
) for
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
, the last two terms on the RHS are bounded by
\langle\tau_{1}-\tau_{1}^{\prime}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)+p-\delta}\big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1}^{\prime},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big)
in view of the proven estimate (

) that for any

,

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+p+C_{j}\delta}\big(F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.137)

We can also add freely
F^{(0)}(k,\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})
to the LHS because of the estimate (
4.74
).
Since the relation
F^{(1)}(k,\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k,\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\gtrsim F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})
holds true,
this energy decay estimate and the decay estimate (
4.4
) together imply that for any
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
and
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(2+j)+p+C_{j}\delta}\Big(F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\Big).

(4.138)

One can see from the above estimates (
4.4
) and (
4.4
) that we have achieved the same energy decay for the energy
F^{(0)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})
for
\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}
. In particular, using the estimates (
4.4
) with

and (
4.4
) with

and adding them together, we have

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k,\delta,\tau_{2},(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+1})+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+C_{j}\delta}\Big(F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),4-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\tilde{\ell}\in\{\mathfrak{s}+1,\geq\mathfrak{s}+2\}}F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\tilde{\ell}})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime}(j),2-\delta,\tau_{1},\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\Big).

(4.139)

The reason that we can add
F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}})
to the LHS is by a simple fact that
F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}{\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}})\lesssim F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime},\delta,\tau_{2},(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+1})
.

Our next goal is to further refine these energy decay for the
\mathfrak{s}
,
\mathfrak{s}+1
and
\geq\mathfrak{s}+2
modes in different ways.

For
\geq\mathfrak{s}+2
modes, we utilize the global
r^{p}
estimate (
•
‣
4.21
) with

. We utilize the estimates (

) for the last fifth and fourth terms, (

) for the last third and second terms and (
4.4
) for the last term on the RHS and bound these last five terms by

\displaystyle C_{k,\delta}\tau_{1}^{-2+\delta+p}\big(

\displaystyle F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},4-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}+1})

\displaystyle+F^{(1)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(0)}(k+k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau_{1},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})\big).

(4.140)

Plugging this estimate back to the global

estimate (
•
‣
4.21
), and using the estimate (

), we conclude for any
p\in[\delta,2-\delta]
,

\displaystyle\hskip-30.1388ptF^{(2)}(k,p,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau_{2},\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k,\delta,\tau_{2},(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+1})+\lVert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(2)})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2}\rVert^{2}_{W_{p-3}^{k}(\mathcal{D}_{\tau_{2},\infty}^{\geq 4M})}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{k,\delta,j}{}

\displaystyle\langle\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}\rangle^{-(2-2\delta)(1+j)+p+C_{j}\delta}\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime}(j)}_{\text{total},\tau_{1}}[\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}].

(4.141)

Since there exists a universal constant
k^{\prime}
such that

\displaystyle F^{(2)}(k,\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(0)}(k,2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k,\delta,\tau,(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+1})

\displaystyle\gtrsim_{k,\delta}F^{(1)}(k-k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+2})+F^{(0)}(k-k^{\prime},2-\delta,\tau,\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\mathfrak{s}})+F^{(1)}(k-k^{\prime},\delta,\tau,(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\Psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{\geq\mathfrak{s}+1}),

(4.142)

then, by using the above energy decay estimate (
4.4
) and the estimate (
4.4
) with
\tilde{\ell}
taking
\geq\mathfrak{s}+2
, we arrive at the estimate (

).

We proceed to the
\mathfrak{s}+1
mode. In the global

) for

, we use again the estimates (
