\displaystyle\begin{split}\bigg|(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\bigg|\lesssim_{\delta,\alpha}{}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}u^{-2-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.37)

Here,

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}=\mathfrak{q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{\rho\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho)\mathrm{d}\tau,

(5.38)

where
\mathfrak{q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
is determined in the initial data assumption
5.6
,
\int_{\tau_{0}}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{\rho\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho)\mathrm{d}\tau
is calculated in (
5.2.1
) with
\mathbf{P}_{m,\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\sin^{2}\theta\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}})
and
\mathbf{P}_{m,\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}})
for a spin
+\mathfrak{s}
scalar
\varphi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
and the constants
b_{m,\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}
and
c_{m,\ell}^{+\mathfrak{s}}
defined as in Proposition
2.13
, and the integral
\int_{\tau_{0}}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{\rho\to+\infty}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho)\mathrm{d}\tau
is calculated in (
5.2
).

Proof.

Step 1. Asymptotics of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}
.

We integrate equation (
5.30
) along constant
v
starting from
\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}
, and by (
5.25
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt\big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\big)(u,v)-\big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\big)(u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v),v)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v)}^{u}\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}\Big(\frac{v^{2}}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)^{\mathfrak{s}+\frac{3}{2}}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}\bigg(\frac{v^{2}}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\bigg)^{\mathfrak{s}+\frac{3}{2}}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{v}^{v^{\prime}}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime\prime})}^{u}\Big(\frac{1}{2}V-\frac{iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)\bigg(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}\bigg(\frac{v^{2}}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\bigg)^{\mathfrak{s}+\frac{3}{2}}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg)(u^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}\cap\{\rho\geq\rho(\tau_{0},v)\}}\frac{1}{2}\mu H_{\text{hyp}}\cdot\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}\bigg(\frac{v^{2}}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\bigg)^{\mathfrak{s}+\frac{3}{2}}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau_{0},\rho)\mathrm{d}\rho

(5.39)

for any
v^{\prime}>v
, and then we take
v^{\prime}\to+\infty
.

Next, we focus on analyzing the RHS of (
5.2.1
).
The first integral in the third last line is equal to

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(+\infty)}^{u}\lim\limits_{v^{\prime}\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(+\infty)}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{v^{\prime}\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\int_{u}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{v^{\prime}\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(+\infty)}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{v^{\prime}\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u^{\prime},v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}+O(u^{-1+C\delta})(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.40)

where we have used the decay estimates in Corollary
4.25
and Proposition
4.26
that imply

\displaystyle\lvert G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\rvert_{k,\mathbb{D}}\lesssim_{\delta,k}u^{-2+C\delta}(\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.41)

for
\delta
sufficiently small. And the second term in the third last line is bounded by
Cv^{(2\mathfrak{s}+3)-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+4)+\delta}(\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime},\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}
by using the estimates (
4.147a
).

For the integral in the second last line of equation (
5.2.1
), its absolute value is bounded by

\displaystyle\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}\Big\{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4}(vr^{-1}+\log(r-r_{+})+u)|G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|+v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3}|VG_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|\Big\}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}.

(5.42)

The first part of (
5.42
) can be estimated by using the decay estimates in Corollary
4.25
and Proposition
4.26
, that is,

\displaystyle\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4}\big(vr^{-1}+\log(r-r_{+})+u\big)|G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+3)}(v^{1-\alpha}+u)r^{-1}|G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\delta}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+1-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+3)}(v^{1-\alpha}+u)u^{-2+\delta}\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\alpha,\delta}{}(v^{3+2\mathfrak{s}-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+4)}+v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+3)+2\delta})(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.43)

To estimate the remaing part in (
5.42
),
by applying Sobolev inequality (
2.41
) to (
4.4
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}+1}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}\lvert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}((\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{m,\ell})\rvert_{k,\mathbb{D}}\lesssim_{k,\delta}\tau^{-\frac{1}{2}+C\delta-j}(\textbf{I}^{k+k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.44)

Thus, combining with (
5.34
), (
5.44
) and Corollary
4.25
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3}|VG_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-5}\Big(\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(rV)^{\leq 1}\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}+1}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(rV)^{\leq 1}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{m,\ell}+\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+2}\Big)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\delta}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},k,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{v}^{+\infty}\int_{u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v^{\prime})}^{u}v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-5}\Big(v^{-1+\delta}+u^{-\frac{1}{2}+C\delta}+u^{-2+\delta}\Big)\mathrm{d}u^{\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\alpha,\delta}{}\Big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+4-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+5)+2\delta}+v^{2\mathfrak{s}+\frac{9}{2}-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+\frac{11}{2}+C\delta)}+v^{2\mathfrak{s}+4-\alpha(2\mathfrak{s}+5)}\Big)(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.45)

In summary, by taking
\delta
sufficiently small and
\alpha
(depending on the value of
\delta)
sufficiently close to
1
, the integral in the second last line of equation (
5.2.1
) is bounded by
v^{-\varepsilon}
for some small
\varepsilon
.

For the integral in the last line of equation (
5.2.1
), by the estimate (
5.41
) and inequality (
5.27
), it is bounded by

\displaystyle C\int_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}},\rho(\tau_{0},v)}r^{-2}(\log(r-r_{+}))^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}|G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}|(\tau_{0},\rho)\mathrm{d}\rho

\displaystyle\lesssim{}\rho(\tau_{0},v)^{-1+\epsilon}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim v^{\alpha(-1+\epsilon)}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.46)

for any
\epsilon\in(0,1)
.

Last, for the second term in the first line of (
5.2.1
), by initial data assumption and (
5.27
), we have

\displaystyle\big|\big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\big)(\tau_{0},v)-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mathfrak{q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\big|\lesssim D_{0}v^{-\varepsilon}.

(5.47)

Combined with the above discussions, we achieve for
\delta
sufficiently small and
\alpha
sufficiently close to
1
, it holds in the region
r\geq v^{\alpha}
that

\displaystyle\Big|(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-1}\mu\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}-(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3}2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mathbb{Q}_{m,+\mathfrak{s}}\Big|\lesssim_{\delta,\alpha}{}v^{-3}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),

(5.48)

with

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}=\mathfrak{q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{\rho\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho)\mathrm{d}\tau.

(5.49)

Step 2. Asymptotics of
(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
.

We first recall the definition of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}
in Proposition
3.19
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mu\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}=

\displaystyle V(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{1}{2(r^{2}+a^{2})}\mu\Big(2iam(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}

\displaystyle\qquad+a^{2}\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+2}c_{m,\ell}^{\mathfrak{s}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\ell}-2ia\mathfrak{s}\sum_{\mathfrak{s}\leq\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+1}b_{m,\ell}^{\mathfrak{s}}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\ell}\Big),

(5.50)

where we have used the mode projection Proposition
2.13
.
Together with (
5.48
) and the almost sharp decay estimates in Proposition
4.26
, this yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\Big|V(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}\Big|\lesssim_{\delta,\alpha}{}\big(v^{-3}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+v^{-1-\alpha}u^{-2+\delta}\big)(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(5.51)

We then derive the asymptotic profile of
(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
. To obtain the asymptotics for
(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
,
one integrates along
u=const
and utilizes (
5.25
) to obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u))+\frac{1}{2}\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}(V-\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}})(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u))+\frac{1}{2}\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\Big((V-\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}})(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}\Big)(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle+2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\frac{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}.

(5.52)

For the last line of (
5.2.1
), one has by (
5.26c
) that

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\frac{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-2\mathfrak{s}}\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}+O(1)\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\frac{r^{2\mathfrak{s}-1}\log r}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime},

(5.53)

and a simple calculation yields

\displaystyle\int\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v)\mathrm{d}v=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}C_{2\mathfrak{s}}^{j}\frac{1}{j-2\mathfrak{s}-2}v^{j-2\mathfrak{s}-2}(-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}C_{2\mathfrak{s}+2}^{j}(j-2\mathfrak{s}-1)v^{j-2\mathfrak{s}-2}(-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}-j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\partial_{v}\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}C_{2\mathfrak{s}+2}^{j}v^{j-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}-j}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}u^{-2}\partial_{v}\Big(\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}C_{2\mathfrak{s}+2}^{j}(\frac{-u}{v})^{2\mathfrak{s}+2-j}v\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}u^{-2}\partial_{v}\Big(v(\frac{v-u}{v})^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}+\frac{u}{2\mathfrak{s}+2}-v\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\Big((\frac{v-u}{v})^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}-1\Big)\frac{1}{u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}.

(5.54)

Thus, we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg|\int_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}^{v}\frac{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}(u,v^{\prime})\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}-2^{-2\mathfrak{s}}\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}\bigg)\bigg|\\
&\lesssim{}(v^{(\alpha-1)(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}u^{-2}+v^{(\alpha-1)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)-1}u^{-1}+v^{-3+\varepsilon})|_{v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u)}\\
&\lesssim{}u^{-2}v^{(\alpha-1)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}.\end{split}

(5.55)

By (
5.51
), the second last integral on the RHS of
(
5.2.1
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\big(v^{-2}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+v^{-\alpha}u^{-2+\delta}+v^{-2\alpha+1}u^{-2+\delta}\big)\big|_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim_{\delta,\alpha}u^{-2-\varepsilon}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.56)

for
\delta
sufficiently small.
For the first term on the RHS of
(
5.2.1
), by using Proposition
4.26
,

\displaystyle\lvert(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(u))\rvert\lesssim{}

\displaystyle r^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}v^{-1-2\mathfrak{s}}u^{-2+\delta^{\prime}}|_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{}

\displaystyle v^{(\alpha-1)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)+2{\delta^{\prime}}}u^{-2-{\delta^{\prime}}}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim{}

\displaystyle u^{-2-\delta^{\prime}}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta^{\prime},\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.57)

by taking
\delta^{\prime}
(depending on the value of
1-\alpha
) sufficiently small.
In summary, by letting
\alpha
sufficiently close to
1
and
\delta=\delta(\alpha)
sufficiently small, there exists an
\varepsilon>0
such that

\displaystyle\bigg|(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg(\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}\bigg)\bigg|\lesssim_{\alpha,\delta}{}

\displaystyle u^{-2-\varepsilon}(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(5.58)

Thus, we complete the proof.
∎

5.2.2.
Sharp decay for derivatives of
(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
in
\{r\geq v^{\alpha}\}
.

We proceed to derive the asymptotic profiles of the derivatives of
(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
in
\{r\geq v^{\alpha}\}
.

Lemma 5.8
.

Let
\mathfrak{s}=0,1,2
,
\lvert m\rvert\leq\mathfrak{s}
, and
j\in\mathbb{N}
. Let the initial data assumption
5.6
to order
j
hold true, and let
j_{1},j_{2},j_{3}\in\mathbb{N}
with
j_{1}+j_{2}+j_{3}\leq j
. Let
\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
be defined as in Lemma
5.7
. Assume
\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}}<+\infty
for a sufficiently small
\delta>0
and some suitably large integer
k
depending on
j
. Then for
\alpha\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
sufficiently close to
1
and
\delta=\delta(\alpha)>0
sufficiently small, there exists an
\varepsilon=\varepsilon(\alpha,\delta)>0
such that in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha}\}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j_{1}}\partial_{v}^{j_{2}}\partial_{u}^{j_{3}}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)(2\mathfrak{s}+2)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\alpha,\delta}{}\sum_{n=0}^{j}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1-j+n}u^{-2-n-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.59)

Proof.

We divide the proof into four steps.

Step 1. Asymptotics of
\tilde{V}
derivatives of
\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}
.

By commuting equation (
5.30
) with
\tilde{V}^{i}
, and because of the commutators (
2.18
) and formula (
5.34
), we have

\displaystyle\hskip-15.0694pt-\mu Y\big(\tilde{V}^{i}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})\big)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\tilde{V}^{i}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-\frac{3}{2}}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(-1)^{i}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-\frac{3}{2}-\frac{i}{2}}\bigg(\frac{(2\mathfrak{s}+3+i-1)!}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)!}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}+O(1)\sum_{n=1}^{i}(rV)^{n}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(-1)^{i}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-\frac{3}{2}-\frac{i}{2}}\bigg\{\frac{(2\mathfrak{s}+3+i-1)!}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)!}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}

\displaystyle+O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(rV)^{\leq i}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}+O(r^{-1})\sum_{\ell=\mathfrak{s}+1}^{\mathfrak{s}+2}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(rV)^{\leq i}(\hat{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s}}^{(1)})_{m,\ell}+O(r^{-1})\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+2}\bigg\}.

(5.60)

Notice that the terms in the last line have faster decay in
r
than the terms in the last second line by (
5.44
) and Corollary
4.25
.

Multiply on both sides of (
5.2.2
) by
v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3+i}
and integrate along constant
v
from the initial hypersurface
\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}
. We apply the same steps used in Step 1 of the proof of Lemma
5.7
and arrive at

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt\big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3+i}\tilde{V}^{i}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})\big)(u,v)

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt-\big(v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3+i}\tilde{V}^{i}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})\big)(u_{\Sigma_{\tau_{0}}}(v),v)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(-1)^{i+1}2^{2\mathfrak{s}+2+i}\frac{(2\mathfrak{s}+2+i)!}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)!}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{+\infty}\lim\limits_{\rho\to+\infty}G_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho)\mathrm{d}\tau+(O(u^{-1+\delta})+O(v^{-\varepsilon}))(\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

(5.61)

for
k^{\prime}=k^{\prime}(i)
large enough and
\delta>0
small enough.
Further, by the initial data assumption, we achieve for any
i\in\mathbb{N}
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hskip 8.61108pt&\hskip-8.61108pt\Big|\tilde{V}^{i}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})\big)(u,v)-\partial_{v}^{i}(v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3})2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3+i}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\Big|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\delta,\alpha}{}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.62)

Step 2. Asymptotics of
\partial_{v}^{i}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
.

We substitute (
5.2.1
) to (
5.62
) with
i=0
. Combined with the basic calculation

\displaystyle\partial_{v}\Big\{\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}\Big\}=\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}},

(5.63)

the estimate (
5.37
) and the expression
\partial_{v}=\frac{1}{2}V-\frac{a}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\eta}
by (
5.25
), we achieve

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg|\partial_{v}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\bigg|\\
&\lesssim_{\alpha,\delta}{}\bigg((v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-2}v^{-\varepsilon}u^{-2-\delta}+(v-u)^{-1}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})\bigg)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.64)

Further, by (
5.62
), we have

\displaystyle(v-u)\bigg|\tilde{V}^{i}\partial_{v}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big|\tilde{V}^{i}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\big|\tilde{V}^{i}\big(O(r^{-2})\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{\leq 1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\ell\leq\mathfrak{s}+2}\big)\big|+v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),

(5.65)

hence, we obtain via a simple iteration that

\displaystyle\bigg|\partial_{v}^{i}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}\Big((v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-i-1}v^{-\varepsilon}u^{-2-\delta}+\sum_{j=1}^{i}(v-u)^{-j}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i+j}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})\Big)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.66)

Step 3. Asymptotics of
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
.
Combining the estimate (
5.62
) and equation (
5.2.2
), and by
2\mathcal{L}_{\xi}=\mu Y+\tilde{V}
, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big|2\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\tilde{V}^{i-1}(\mu^{\mathfrak{s}+1}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}-1}\tilde{\Phi}_{+\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}})(u,v)-\partial_{v}^{i}(v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3})2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3+i}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\Big|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}\bigg(v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-2-i}u^{-2+\delta}\bigg)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.67)

Repeating the above process yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\Big\{(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}}\big[(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-1}\mu\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}(u,v)-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\big]\Big\}\Big|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}\bigg(v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}r^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j}u^{-2-(i-1-j)+\delta}\bigg)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),\end{split}

(5.68)

which is equivalent to

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\bigg((r^{2}+a^{2})^{-1}\mu\tilde{\Phi}_{\mathfrak{s},m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}\bigg(r^{2\mathfrak{s}}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}r^{-3-j}u^{-2-(i-1-j)+\delta}\bigg)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime},\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.69)

Similar to Step 2, we combine the estimate (
5.2.1
) and the almost sharp pointwise decay estimates in Proposition
4.26
together to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg|\tilde{V}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\Big\{(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}\Big)\Big\}\bigg|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}\bigg(v^{-3-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+v^{-1-\alpha}u^{-2-i+\delta}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}v^{-\alpha(3+j)}u^{-1-i+j+\delta}\bigg)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.70)

By integrating the above inequality along
u
-constant hypersurface from
\gamma_{\alpha}
, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\Big\{(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{s}+1}\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u}\Big)\Big\}\bigg|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}\Big(r^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}u^{-2-i+\delta}|_{\gamma_{\alpha}}+\sum_{j=0}^{i}(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}+1}u^{-1-j}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-2+j-i}|_{\gamma_{\alpha}}\\
{}&\quad+v^{-2-i}(v^{-\varepsilon}+u^{-1+\delta})+v^{-\alpha}u^{-2-i+\delta}+\sum_{j=0}^{i-1}v^{-\alpha(3+j)+1}u^{-1-i+j+\delta}\Big|_{\gamma_{\alpha}}\Big)((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0})\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}u^{-2-i-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.71)

Therefore, we achieve

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\Big\{(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-4\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big\}\bigg|\\
&\lesssim_{i,\alpha,\delta}{}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}u^{-2-i-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k^{\prime}(i),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).\end{split}

(5.72)

Step 4. Asymptotics of
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\partial_{v}^{j}\partial_{u}^{k}(\Phi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
.
Similar to proving (
5.68
), we can derive the asymptotics for
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\tilde{V}^{k}
derivatives, and these imply (
5.8
) for
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i}\partial_{v}^{j}
. Finally, using
\partial_{u}=\mathcal{L}_{\xi}-\partial_{v}
, we complete the proof.
∎

5.2.3.
Sharp decay for the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components in
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
.

Given the above asymptotics for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component, one can derive the asymptotics for the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component via the TSI in Section
3.4
. We state the asymptotics of both of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components in region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
, for some
\alpha^{\prime}\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
, in the following theorem.

Theorem 5.9

(Asymptotics of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
component in
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
)
.

Let
\mathfrak{s}=0,1,2
and let
\lvert m\rvert\leq\mathfrak{s}
. Let
\lvert a\rvert/M<1
for
\mathfrak{s}=0
and let
\lvert a\rvert/M\ll 1
sufficiently small. Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
and
\lvert\mathbf{a}\rvert=j
and
\mathbb{P}=\{\mathcal{L}_{\xi},\partial_{u},\partial_{v}\}
. Let
\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
be defined as in Lemma
5.7
. Assume the initial data condition
5.6
to order
j+2\mathfrak{s}
hold true, and
\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}}<+\infty
for a sufficiently small
\delta>0
and some suitably large integer
k
depending on
j+2\mathfrak{s}
. Then, there exists an
\alpha^{\prime}\in(\frac{1}{2},1)
sufficiently close to
1
and an
\varepsilon=\varepsilon(\alpha^{\prime},\delta)>0
sufficiently small such that
in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}\bigg((r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)\tau}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}\tau^{2}}\sum_{|m|\leq\mathfrak{s}}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\alpha^{\prime},\delta}{}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}\tau^{-2-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0})

(5.73)

and

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}\bigg({\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)(2\mathfrak{s}+2)}\frac{\tau+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v}{\tau^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}v^{2}}\sum_{|m|\leq\mathfrak{s}}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\alpha^{\prime},\delta}{}v^{-1}\tau^{-2-j-2\mathfrak{s}-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.74)

Moreover, the above statement holds for
\lvert a\rvert/M<1
in the cases
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
under the BEAM estimates assumption
4.2
.

Proof.

Take
\alpha^{\prime}\in(\alpha,1)
to be determined. First, in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}\cap\{r\geq\frac{v}{4}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{j}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1-j+k}u^{-2-k-\varepsilon}\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{j}v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1-j+k}u^{-2-k-\varepsilon}\lesssim v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1}u^{-2-j-\varepsilon}.

(5.75)

Next, in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}\cap\{r\leq\frac{v}{4}\}
, there exists an
\varepsilon^{\prime}=\varepsilon^{\prime}(\alpha^{\prime})>0
such that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{j}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}-1-j+k}u^{-2-k-\varepsilon}\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{j}v^{\alpha^{\prime}(-2\mathfrak{s}-1-j+k)}v^{-2-k-\varepsilon}\lesssim v^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon^{\prime}},

(5.76)

by taking
\alpha^{\prime}
sufficiently close to
1
. Together with (
5.8
) for the asymptotics of each
(m,\mathfrak{s})
mode and the pointwise decay estimates (
4.145b
) for
\geq\mathfrak{s}+1
modes, the estimate (
5.9
) follows.

It remains to consider the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component for
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
. As mentioned already, the asymptotics of the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component can be calculated explicitly from the TSI (
3.196b
) and (
3.198b
) and the already proven asymptotics of the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component.
The TSI (
3.196b
) and (
3.198b
) for
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
can be written as

\displaystyle(\mathring{\eth}+ia\sin\theta\mathcal{L}_{\xi})^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}+12M(\mathfrak{s}-1)\overline{\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}}={}

\displaystyle Y^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}),

(5.77)

which can further be expanded and rewritten in the following form

\displaystyle(\mathring{\eth})^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}={}

\displaystyle Y^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i_{1}\geq 1,i_{1}+i_{2}\leq 2\mathfrak{s}}O(1)\mathring{\eth}^{i_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i_{1}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}-12M(\mathfrak{s}-1)\overline{\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}}.

(5.78)

The last two terms in TSI (
5.78
) are with
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}
-derivative and hence have (at least) faster
\tau^{-1+\varepsilon}
decay than
\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}
. Meanwhile,
one can expand
Y^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}=2^{2\mathfrak{s}}\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}+O(r^{-1})\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}+\sum\limits_{i\leq 2\mathfrak{s}-1}O(r^{-2})\partial_{u}^{i}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
by
\mu Y=2\partial_{u}
from (
5.25
) and the terms
O(r^{-1})\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}+\sum\limits_{i\leq 2\mathfrak{s}-1}O(r^{-2})\partial_{u}^{i}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
clearly have faster decay than the term
2^{2\mathfrak{s}}\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
. As a result, by projecting the above TSI (
5.78
) onto an
(m,\mathfrak{s})
mode, one finds

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}}}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}(\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}})\bigg|

\displaystyle={}\bigg|\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}}}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}((v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}(v-u)^{-2\mathfrak{s}}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\alpha^{\prime},\delta}v^{-1}u^{-2-j-2\mathfrak{s}-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.79)

In view of the estimate (
5.9
) and the pointwise decay estimates (
4.151b
) for
\geq\mathfrak{s}+1
modes of the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component,
this yields

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}{\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\mathbb{P}^{\mathbf{a}}\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}(v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u)}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big)\sum_{|m|\leq\mathfrak{s}}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\alpha^{\prime},\delta}{}v^{-1}u^{-2-j-2\mathfrak{s}-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.80)

In the end, by elementary calculations, one has

\displaystyle\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}(v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u)}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big)={}

\displaystyle\frac{(2\mathfrak{s})!((2\mathfrak{s}+1)v+u)}{v^{2}u^{2+2\mathfrak{s}}},

(5.81)

then substituting this into (
5.2.3
)
proves (
5.9
).
∎

5.2.4.
Sharp decay for the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components in the region
\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}

In contrast to the approach in the region
\{r\geq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
that the asymptotics for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component are first derived and the ones for the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component then follow from the TSI, our argument begins with deriving the asymptotics for
the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component, and these yield the asymptotics for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component via the other TSI of Section
3.4
.

The asymtotics of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
components in the region
\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
are provided in the following theorem.

Theorem 5.10

(Asymptotics of the spin
\pm\mathfrak{s}
component in
\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
)
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
, and
\mathfrak{s}=0,1,2
. Let
\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
be defined as in Lemma
5.7
. Let
\alpha^{\prime}
be chosen as in Theorem
5.9
. Assume for each
m
with
\lvert m\rvert\leq\mathfrak{s}
, the initial data assumption
5.6
to order
j+2\mathfrak{s}
hold true, and
\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}}<+\infty
for a sufficiently small
\delta>0
and a suitably large integer
k
depending on
j+2\mathfrak{s}
.
Then, there exists an
\varepsilon>0
such that
in the region
\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
,

\displaystyle\hskip-12.91663pt\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\bigg(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}-\sum_{|m|\leq\mathfrak{s}}\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{-\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\tau^{-3-2\mathfrak{s}-j}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}{}\tau^{-3-2\mathfrak{s}-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),

(5.82a)

\displaystyle\hskip-12.91663pt\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\bigg((r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}-\sum_{|m|\leq\mathfrak{s}}\mathfrak{f}_{+\mathfrak{s},m}\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\tau^{-3-2\mathfrak{s}-j}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),

(5.82b)

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{f}_{+\mathfrak{s},m}=

\displaystyle\mu^{\mathfrak{s}}+\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\sum_{n=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(C_{2\mathfrak{s}}^{n}-\delta_{-\mathfrak{s},n})u_{-\mathfrak{s},n}(r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times(r-M)^{2\mathfrak{s}-n}(r^{2}+a^{2})^{n-\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\mu\partial_{\rho}+\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)^{n-1}\Big(\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)

(5.82c)

with
u_{-\mathfrak{s},n}(r)
and
\delta_{-\mathfrak{s},n}
as defined in Lemma
5.1
and
f_{+\mathfrak{s},m}=\mu^{\mathfrak{s}}+amO(r^{-1})
.

Further, if
\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}

(\mathfrak{s}\neq 0)
is supported on an azimuthal
m
-mode, then on
\mathcal{H}^{+}
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\bigg(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\big|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}-\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)(2\mathfrak{s})!}\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\sum_{n=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(C_{2\mathfrak{s}}^{n}-\delta_{-\mathfrak{s},n})(r_{+}-M)^{2\mathfrak{s}-n}({2iam})^{n}\times\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}),

(5.82d)

and for
am=0
, the decay is faster by
\tau^{-1}
:

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\big({\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}\big|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}-D\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k,\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0})

(5.82e)

with the constants
D
being explicitly calculated as in the proof.

Meanwhile, all the statements in this theorem are valid for
\lvert a\rvert/M<1
in the cases
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
under the BEAM estimates assumption
4.2
.

Proof.

Consider first the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component
(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
. We have achieved in Proposition
4.26
that

\displaystyle\lvert\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{\rho}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\rvert\lesssim_{j,\delta}{}

\displaystyle v^{-1}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j+\delta}(\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.83)

for
\delta
sufficiently small.
For any point
(\tau,\rho^{\prime})\in\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
, we integrate
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{\rho}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
from point
(\tau,\rho^{\prime})
along constant
\tau
up to the intersection point with the curve
\gamma_{\alpha^{\prime}}
, thus, it holds

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(\tau,\rho^{\prime})-\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\big|_{\gamma_{\alpha^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq{}\bigg|\int_{\rho^{\prime}}^{\rho_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\tau)}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{\rho}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\mathrm{d}\rho\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta}{}(\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4-j+\delta}\int_{\rho^{\prime}}^{\rho_{\gamma_{\alpha}}(\tau)}\mathrm{d}\rho

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4-j+\delta+\alpha^{\prime}}(\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}(\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.84)

where we have used
(
5.83
) in the second step and chosen

\delta
small enough in the last step.
By the sharp decay estimate (
5.9
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\big|_{\gamma_{\alpha^{\prime}}}={}

\displaystyle\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\mathbb{Q}_{m,+\mathfrak{s}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{u}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\Big(\frac{(v-u)^{2\mathfrak{s}}(v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u)}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\Big)\bigg|_{\gamma_{\alpha^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{2^{2\mathfrak{s}+3}\mathbb{Q}_{m,+\mathfrak{s}}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+2)(2\mathfrak{s}+1)}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\bigg(\frac{v+(2\mathfrak{s}+1)u}{v^{2\mathfrak{s}+2}u^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{\gamma_{\alpha^{\prime}}}+O(\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4+\alpha^{\prime}})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{j}2^{4\mathfrak{s}+3}(2\mathfrak{s}+2+j)!\mathbb{Q}_{m,+\mathfrak{s}}}{(2\mathfrak{s}+1)(2\mathfrak{s}+2)!}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j}+O(\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4+\alpha^{\prime}})

(5.85)

where we have used
v=\tau+O(\tau^{\alpha^{\prime}})
and
u=\tau+O(\tau^{\alpha^{\prime}})
on
\gamma_{\alpha}^{\prime}
.
Substituting this back into (
5.2.4
), and in view of the faster decay estimates (
4.151b
) for
\geq\mathfrak{s}+1
modes, we hence prove (
5.82
) in
\{r\leq v^{\alpha^{\prime}}\}
.

Consider next the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
component. We can obtain its asymptotics by utilizing the TSI (
3.196a
) and (
3.198a
) and the above estimates for the spin
-\mathfrak{s}
component. Recall the TSI for
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
:

\displaystyle\ (\mathring{\eth}^{\prime}-ia\sin\theta\mathcal{L}_{\xi})^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}-12M(\mathfrak{s}-1)\overline{\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}={}

\displaystyle\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\hat{V}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}),

(5.86)

which can again be expanded and written as

\displaystyle(\mathring{\eth}^{\prime})^{2\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}={}

\displaystyle\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\hat{V}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})+\sum_{i_{1}\geq 1,i_{1}+i_{2}\leq 2\mathfrak{s}}O(1)(\mathring{\eth}^{\prime})^{i_{2}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{i_{1}}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}+12M(\mathfrak{s}-1)\overline{\mathcal{L}_{\xi}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}}.

(5.87)

By (
•
‣
5.1
), we can expand out the
(m,\mathfrak{s})
mode of the first term on the RHS as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\hat{V}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(\Delta^{\mathfrak{s}}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}}))=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\tilde{u}_{-\mathfrak{s},j}(r)\mathbf{P}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}(V^{j}\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\tilde{u}_{-\mathfrak{s},j}(r)(\mu\partial_{\rho}+\mu H_{\text{hyp}}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}+\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}})^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big((r^{2}+a^{2})^{\mathfrak{s}}u_{-\mathfrak{s},0}(r)+\sum_{j=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\tilde{u}_{-\mathfrak{s},j}(r)(\mu\partial_{\rho}+\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}})^{j-1}\frac{2iam}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)\cdot(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j+k\leq 2\mathfrak{s}-1}b_{j,k}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\mu r\partial_{\rho})^{k}\partial_{\rho}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}+\sum_{j=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}c_{j}\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}.

(5.88)

The last line of (
5.2.4
) has faster
\tau^{-1}
decay by the decay estimates in Proposition
4.26
;
moreover, the last two terms with
\mathcal{L}_{\xi}
-derivatives on the RHS of (
5.87
) also have faster decay in
\tau
. Thus, a projection onto
(m,\mathfrak{s})
mode for the TSI (
5.87
) yields

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}((r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}})-\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\Big(u_{-\mathfrak{s},0}(r)+(r^{2}+a^{2})^{-\mathfrak{s}}\sum_{i=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}\tilde{u}_{-\mathfrak{s},i}(r)(\mu\partial_{\rho}+\frac{2aim}{r^{2}+a^{2}})^{i-1}\frac{2aim}{r^{2}+a^{2}}\Big)\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.89)

Substituting the asymptotic estimate (
5.82
) of
(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}
and the definition of
\tilde{u}_{-\mathfrak{s},i}(r)
in Lemma
5.1
into the above inequality and by the decay estimates (
4.150b
) for the
\geq\mathfrak{s}+1
modes of
\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
, we obtain (
5.82
). Meanwhile, it is manifest by the expression (
5.82
) of
\mathfrak{f}_{+\mathfrak{s},m}
that
\mathfrak{f}_{+\mathfrak{s},m}=\mu^{\mathfrak{s}}+amO(r^{-1})
.

Last, we discuss the sharp decay on the event horizon for the spin
+\mathfrak{s}
components for
\mathfrak{s}=1,2
. Restricting (
5.87
) on
\mathcal{H}^{+}
, see for example (
5.15
), then we get

\displaystyle\hskip-8.61108pt\bigg|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}-\frac{1}{(2\mathfrak{s})!}\sum_{n=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(C_{2\mathfrak{s}}^{n}-\delta_{-\mathfrak{s},n})(r_{+}-M)^{2\mathfrak{s}-n}(2iam)^{n}(\psi_{-\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\bigg|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}{}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.90)

Substituting (
5.82
) into the above inequality and by the decay estimates (
4.150b
) for the
(m,\geq\mathfrak{s}+1)
modes of
\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
, we achieve (
5.10
). Further, it can be easily check that the coefficient
\sum_{n=1}^{2\mathfrak{s}}(C_{2\mathfrak{s}}^{n}-\delta_{-\mathfrak{s},n})(r_{+}-M)^{2\mathfrak{s}-n}(2iam)^{n}
in fact vanishes if and only if
am=0
. Therefore, we have in the case
am=0
that

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}\big|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-3-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.91)

We can use again the TSI (
5.15
) and substitute in the above estimate, and this then yields

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}\big((\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\big|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}-D\mathbb{Q}_{m,\mathfrak{s}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4}Y_{m,\mathfrak{s}}^{+\mathfrak{s}}(\cos\theta)e^{im{\tilde{\phi}}}\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0})

(5.92)

where
D
can be calculated explicitly from the TSI (
5.15
).
By projecting the TSI (
5.87
) on
(m,\geq\mathfrak{s}+1)
modes and restricting on
\mathcal{H}^{+}
, one finds the last two terms on the RHS are
O(\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-\frac{9}{2}+C\delta})
by the above estimate (
5.92
) and the decay estimates (
4.150b
) for the
(m,\geq\mathfrak{s}+1)
modes of
\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}}
, and the first term on the RHS is bounded as well by
O(\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-\frac{9}{2}+C\delta})
using (
5.6
) and the decay estimates (
4.151b
). Hence, by taking
\delta
sufficiently small, we arrive at

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{L}_{\xi}^{j}(\psi_{+\mathfrak{s}})_{m,\geq\mathfrak{s}+1}\big|_{\mathcal{H}^{+}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{j,\delta,\alpha^{\prime}}\tau^{-2\mathfrak{s}-4-j-\varepsilon}((\textbf{I}^{k(j),\delta,\pm\mathfrak{s}}_{\text{total},\tau_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}+D_{0}).

(5.93)

Combining this estimate with (
5.92
) then proves
the estimate (
5.82e
).
∎
